=== RELEASE v0.54.0 | 2026-10-23 | https://github.com/wandb/weave/releases/tag/v0.54.0
## Breaking changes

- `weave.shared` and the `weave.trace_server` alias modules are removed. Importing them now raises `ModuleNotFoundError`; 0.53.13 warned on these imports with `FutureWarning`. (#8061)
- An op saved with code that imports from `weave.shared` loads as `None` in this version. (#8061)

## Deprecations

- `weave.trace_server_bindings.stainless_remote_http_trace_server` is deprecated. Import `RemoteHTTPTraceServer` from `weave.trace_server_bindings.remote_http_trace_server` instead. (#8074)

## What's Changed
* refactor(weave)!: delete weave.shared and the weave.trace_server aliases by @ro31337 in https://github.com/wandb/weave/pull/8061
* refactor(weave): name the Stainless client RemoteHTTPTraceServer by @ro31337 in https://github.com/wandb/weave/pull/8074


**Full Changelog**: https://github.com/wandb/weave/compare/v0.53.13...v0.54.0
